# Changes against witnesses C and H

Counts are distinct recorded loci, not word-error rates. A locus can differ from both witnesses and then contributes to both C and H columns. Confidence columns count each locus once. Purely equivalent macro syntax is excluded. H description-only pages have no H collation rows. C doubt marks resolved or changed count against C; retained doubts are separately recorded.

## Consequential corrections

- Scan 12, Item 2 opening, superscript after S(X): The displayed source of Prol_X bears a vee-shaped superscript, not C’s tilde. The ink is preserved; whether this is intended notation or an authorial slip remains unresolved.
- Scan 23, Cancelled first (ii): family formula blanks: The cancelled family expression has empty arrow and membership slots; H supplies both relations. The consolidated text preserves the incomplete mathematical notation instead of completing it.
- Scan 24, Insertion between est and localement: d. torsion and terminal punctuation: The inserted property reads d. torsion, whereas H reads cf. fonction; the literal property wording is restored without expanding d. in the transcription.
- Scan 24, Item a parenthetical resp. quasi-séparé and its reference: H omits the written parenthetical resp. quasi-séparé case. The consolidated reading retains that case and its separate reference slot.
- Scan 25, Top note, final reference digits: The top claim’s reference is resolved as VI 1.25 rather than an uncertain closing after 1.2.
- Scan 25, Cancelled proof, inserted reference (2.6 (ii)): The finite-product clause cites item (ii), whereas H cites (i).
- Scan 25, Cancelled proof, item i after uncertain numeral: The finite-inductive-limit clause cites item (i), whereas H gives (ii); the preceding numeral remains 6/8.
- Scan 30, P1 corps de insertion: attachment before base: The insertion call qualifies the later base-change expression with corps de; H attaches these words to the earlier au cas du clause. This is an observed attachment difference, not a verification of the reduction.
- Scan 30, P2 Au lieu de pro-lisse through par exemple: cancellation extent: The proposed replacement of pro-lisse/lisse by the asphericity condition belongs to the cancelled text; H presents most of that proposal as live.

The three readers differ materially at the first underlined count, preferred VI B overprint order, Appendice undertrace preference, Saarbach certainty, the cancelled r after mise, and frame geometry; the rows above preserve their alternatives. The three encodings of the ei/ie transposition convey the same ink. Changes in doubt state are included in the counts and are not all categorical witness errors.

### Remaining doubts

| Scan | Place | Reading | Alternatives | Confidence | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 30 | First SGA reference, overprint | SGA \unsure{\deleted{V}4\|\deleted{4}V} XVI 1.1 | \deleted{V}4; \deleted{4}V | medium | Both components survive; their chronological order is not established. |
| 30 | After second th., low mark | \unsure{\handwritten{,}} | Intentional handwritten comma; Incidental ink | low | Comma-shaped head and faint tail are visible, but their intentional punctuation status is unresolved. |
| 30 | Initial of envers | \unsure{\(\mathbb{e}\)\|\deleted{E}e\|\deleted{e}E} | \mathbb{e}; \deleted{E}e; \deleted{e}E | medium | Displaced rounded contours and a possible capital stem/bar do not establish the underlying letters or strike order uniquely. |
| 30 | Period after cancelled rappels | rappels\unsure{.} | . | low | The short oblique cancellation obscures the stop cell. |
| 30 | Raised addition above L-asphérique | \unsure{1-\|l-} | 1-; l- | medium | Upper flag/serif and lower foot are compatible with both impressions. |
| 30 | First marginal phrase | \unsure{si on utilise} | si on utilise | medium | Initial clipping and compressed cursive leave the phrase tentative. |
| 30 | Separated word after utilise | \unsure{un\|en\|on} | un; en; on | low | A separate short cursive word is visible; its rounded initial and final arch are not uniquely discriminated. |
| 30 | Marginal canonique, initial only | \unsure{c}anonique | canonique | medium | The initial curve meets the physical sheet edge; following anonique is clear. |

r1 supplies the graphic geometry, semantic cancellation wrappers, separate underlines and rotated marginal placement. Main adds the r2/r3 SGA and envers overprint alternatives, the r3 uncertain low comma and semicolon, the r2 extra marginal word and faint short stroke, the r2/r3 uncertain period after rappels, and the r3 broken lower stroke. Main resolves the initial dash and typed 5, retains all source errors and incomplete text, and records every C mark. Mathematical checking is explicitly limited to the affine implication.
